# Credential exposure inventory

No matched values or source excerpts are included. Fingerprints use an ephemeral per-run HMAC key, not an unsalted password digest. Full reachable Git history is retained; no rewrite occurred. ACTIVE_PRODUCTION_EXPOSURE means material in the owner-identified deployed source snapshot, not proof that any password is still valid. Production runtime state is not independently inspected.

Scanned 1119 tracked/explicit candidate files and 8712 reachable Git blobs. Locally available owner-supplied production tree: True.

Current classifications: {'FALSE_POSITIVE': 4, 'TEST_PLACEHOLDER': 8}.

Reviewed placeholders: the listed legacy UI fixtures use synthetic accounts/disposable manifests; the price helper uses guarded test DB access; the smoke-test URI and local setup URI are examples. These are not production account credentials. They are not proof every legacy test tool is hardened against arbitrary operator misuse. False positives are UI password error messages, not credential assignments.

Limitations: regex-based scanning is not a proof against every possible encoding/secret format. No current provider/environment secret was retrieved for value comparison. Removed secrets can remain in Git deletion hunks/history; do not share raw diffs or historical source archives. Initial and final inventory JSON preserve separate observations.
